New.Curve.PeriodDefs

Definition 114.1src/New/Curve/PeriodDefs.lean: Parameters.vacuumRoot
Let $P$ be a parameter set. Let $s \in \Natural$. Then $\ParametersvacuumRootMACRoCJS {s}$ is a complex defined as $\InvinvMACRoFW {\mathrm{chiFin\_\_app} (\ParametersubMACRoCIQ {s})}^ {\InvinvMACRoFW {\numInfPlacesMACRoEIW {\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s}}}}$.
The principal f-th root of the complex number υ(α)⁻¹|α|^(1/2), where α = `Parameters.α` and f is the number of infinite places of E.
Theorem 114.2src/New/Curve/PeriodDefs.lean: Parameters.norm_gal_vacuumRoot
Let $P$ be a parameter set. Let $s \in \Natural$. Let $\sigma$ be an algebra isomorphism of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Then $\NormnormMACRoAK {\sigma (\ParametersvacuumRootMACRoCJS {s})}^ {2}$ is equal to the quotient of the root discriminant of $\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s}$ and $\RealsqrtMACRoHU {\ParametersdMACRoCIY}$.
Every automorphism σ of ℂ over K preserves the absolute value of s₀ = `Parameters.vacuumRoot`: |σ(s₀)|² = rootDiscr(E)/√d.
Proof. By Parameters.prod_varpi_eq_rootDiscr_pow, Parameters.vacuumRoot, WeilData.exists_chiFin_pow_eq_prod, toAdele, Parameters.α, ideleAbs_eq_prod_of_v_eq_one, Parameters.v_alpha_mul_relDifferentGen, Parameters.infinitePlace_apply_varpi, ideleAbs_toAdele, Parameters.relDifferentGen_ne_zero, Parameters.prod_phi_eq_norm, finIdeleIncl, chiFin
and 10 lemmas of Mathlib norm_pow, pow_right_comm, pow_left_inj₀, AlgEquiv.commutes, map_pow, Circle.norm_coe, LT.lt.ne', div_pow, Finset.prod_pow, Finset.prod_nonneg
. ∎
Definition 114.3src/New/Curve/PeriodDefs.lean: Parameters.cmCurve
Let $P$ be a parameter set. Then $\ParameterscmCurveMACRoCIX$ is an element of $\CMLatticeMACRoPH {\ParametersdMACRoCIY}$ defined as $\mathrm{Exists\_\_choose} (\mathrm{Parameters\_\_cmCurve\_\_\_proof\_1} (P))$.
A chosen CM datum whose lattice is a complex multiple Ω₀𝓞_K of 𝓞_K: the curve 𝓔 = ℂ/Ω₀𝓞_K.
Definition 114.4src/New/Curve/PeriodDefs.lean: Parameters.Ω₀
Let $P$ be a parameter set. Then $\ParametersuauMACRoCIP$ is a unit of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ defined as $\mathrm{Exists\_\_choose} (\mathrm{Parameters\_\_\_u3a9\_\_u2080\_\_\_\_proof\_1} (P))$.
A chosen nonzero complex number with the lattice of 𝓔 equal to Ω₀𝓞_K.
Definition 114.5src/New/Curve/PeriodDefs.lean: Parameters.areaScale
Let $P$ be a parameter set. Then $\ParametersareaScaleMACRoCIT$ is a unit of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ defined as $\mathrm{Units\_\_mk0} (\ComplexofRealMACRoFDV {\RealsqrtMACRoHU {(\frac{ 2 \times \RealpiMACRoAN}{\RealsqrtMACRoHU {\ParametersdMACRoCIY}})}}, \mathrm{Parameters\_\_areaScale\_\_\_proof\_2} (P))$.
The number (2π/√d)^{1/2}, as a unit of ℂ.
Definition 114.6src/New/Curve/PeriodDefs.lean: Parameters.periodFactor
Let $P$ be a parameter set. Let $s \in \Natural$. Then $\ParametersperiodFactorMACRoCJI {s}$ is a complex defined as $\frac{ \ParametersvacuumRootMACRoCJS {s}}{\mathrm{Exists\_\_choose} (\mathrm{Parameters\_\_periodFactor\_\_\_proof\_6} (P))}$.
The number s₁ = s₀/u₀, with s₀ = `Parameters.vacuumRoot` and u₀ the unit chosen from `CMLattice.chowla_selberg` for the curve 𝓔 (`Parameters.cmCurve`).
Definition 114.7src/New/Curve/PeriodDefs.lean: Parameters.jetScale
Let $P$ be a parameter set. Let $s \in \Natural$. Then $\ParametersjetScaleMACRoCJG {s}$ is a complex defined as $\frac{ \ParametersperiodFactorMACRoCJI {s}\times \UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\ParametersareaScaleMACRoCIT}}{\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\ParametersuauMACRoCIP}}$.
The number per = s₁(2π/√d)^{1/2}/Ω₀, the scale at which Taylor series of theta functions are taken.

New.Curve.ThetaProducts

Theorem 114.8src/New/Curve/ThetaProducts.lean: sum_pow_eq_zero_of_null
Let $p , B , e \in \Natural$. Let $Q$ be a commutative ring. Let $J$ be a finite type. Assume that $p$ is prime. Assume that $p$ is characteristic. Let $X$ be a proposition. Let $t$ and $L$ be functions from $J$ to $Q$. Assume that both $e = 1$ and $X$ or $e = 2$. Assume that for all elements $i_{4}$ of $J$, $\EqtMACRoDE {t (i_{4})- t (i_{4})^ {p ^ {e}}}{L (i_{4})}$. Assume that for all elements $i_{3}$ of $J$, for all $n \geq B$ in $\Natural$, $\EqtMACRoDE {t (i_{3})^ {p ^ {n}}}{0}$. Assume that for all elements $j$ of $\Natural$, if $j$ is odd or $X$, then $\EqtMACRoDE {\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{i_{2}}{(L (i_{2})^ {p ^ {j}}\times L (i_{2}))}}{0}$. Then $\EqtMACRoDE {\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{i}{(t (i)^ {(p + 1)})}}{0}$ and $\EqtMACRoDE {\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{i_{1}}{(t (i_{1})^ {(p + p ^ {e})})}}{0}$.
In characteristic p, let e = 2, or e = 1 and ρ hold; let t_i − t_i^{p^e} = L_i and t_i^{p^n} = 0 for n ≥ B. If ∑L_i^{p^j+1} = 0 for odd j (all j if ρ), then ∑t_i^{p+1} = 0 and ∑t_i^{p+p^e} = 0.
Proof. By eq_sum_pow_of_sub_pow_eq
and 10 lemmas of Mathlib pow_succ, pow_add, Nat.Prime.pos, pow_mul, add_eq_left, mul_comm, Finset.sum_comm, mul_pow, sum_pow_char, Finset.sum_mul_sum
. ∎
Theorem 114.9src/New/Curve/ThetaProducts.lean: coeff_sum_frob_ne_zero_iff
Let $p \in \Natural$. Let $Q$ be a commutative ring. Let $J$ and $X$ be finite types. Assume that $p$ is prime. Assume that $p$ is characteristic. Let $I$ be a set. Let $r \in X \rightarrow J \rightarrow Q$. Let $t$ be a function from elements of $J$ to multivariate power series of $\ProdMACRoEZ {X}{I}$ and $Q$ such that for all elements $i_{3}$ of $J$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\mathrm{MvPowerSeries\_\_constantCoeff\_\_app} (t (i_{3}))}{0}$. Let $x_{3} \in X$. Let $c \in I$ such that for all elements $i_{2}$ of $J$, $\EqtMACRoDE {t (i_{2})- t (i_{2})^ {p ^ {2}}}{\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{v}{x_{5}}{(\HMulhMulappMACRoJO {\mathrm{MvPowerSeries\_\_C\_\_app} (r (v , i_{2}))}{\mathrm{MvPowerSeries\_\_X} (\ProdmkMACRoDR {v}{c})}{x_{5}})}}$. Let $d \in \FinsuppMACRoDN {(\ProdMACRoEZ {X}{I})}{\Natural}$. Then $\mathrm{MvPowerSeries\_\_coeff\_\_app} (d , \FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{i}{x_{4}}{(\HMulhMulappMACRoJO {(t (i)^ {p})}{\mathrm{MvPowerSeries\_\_C\_\_app} (r (x_{3} , i))}{x_{4}})})\neq 0$, if and only if there exists a natural number $n$, such that there exists an element $y$ of $X$, such that $\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{i_{1}}{(r (y , i_{1})^ {p ^ {(2 \times n + 1)}}\times r (x_{3} , i_{1}))}\neq 0$ and $d$ is equal to $\FinsuppsingleMACRoDP {(\ProdmkMACRoDR {y}{c})}{(p ^ {(2 \times n + 1)})}$.
Over a commutative ring of prime characteristic p, let power series t_i have t_i(0) = 0, t_i − t_i^{p²} = ∑_v r_{v,i}X_{v,c}. Then ∑_i r_{x,i}t_i^p has nonzero coefficients exactly at the X_{y,c}^{p^j} with j odd and ∑_i r_{y,i}^{p^j}r_{x,i} ≠ 0.
Proof. By eq_sum_pow_of_sub_pow_eq
and 16 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.exists_ne_zero_of_sum_ne_zero, Finsupp.single_eq_single_iff, MvPowerSeries.coeff_X_pow, Nat.Prime.one_lt, MvPowerSeries.smul_eq_C_mul, MvPowerSeries.coeff_eq_zero_of_constantCoeff_nilpotent, pow_succ, add_pow_char, charP_of_injective_ringHom, pow_mul, Finset.sum_eq_single, Finset.sum_product', Finsupp.degree_single, MvPowerSeries.C_injective, Finset.sum_comm, Finset.sum_mul
. ∎
Definition 114.10src/New/Curve/ThetaProducts.lean: Parameters.thetaProducts
Let $p , s \in \Natural$. Let $P$ be a parameter set. Let $\delta \in \NumberFieldRingOfIntegersMACRoFDD {(\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ParametersKMACRoCIK}{x_{2}})})}$. Let $r \in \Real$. Let $c$ be a set. Then $\ParametersthetaProductsMACRoCJQ {s}{\delta}{r}{c}$ is an element of $\wp \mathrm{MvPowerSeries} (\ProdMACRoEZ {\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace} (\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s})}{c}, \mathrm{PadicComplex} (p))$.
The set of series ∏ι(U_{y_{j,k}}θ)(ι(b w_j†)z_k) over ℂ_p, over j and k ∈ c, with (w_j) a tight frame of length M, |ιb| = r, nδy_{j,k} ∈ Λ, p prime to Mn; U_y = `CMLattice.twistedTranslate` 1 y, and θ, Λ are those of `Parameters.cmCurve`.
Theorem 114.11src/New/Curve/ThetaProducts.lean: Parameters.exists_frame_rows_frob
Let $p , s , M \in \Natural$. Let $J$ be a finite type. Assume that $p$ is prime. Let $P$ be a parameter set. Assume that $\PadicPlaceMACRoCGW {p}$. Assume that $\mathrm{chiK\_\_app} (\ParametersdMACRoCIY , p)\neq 1$. Let $w$ be a function from $J$ to $\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s}$ such that $\IsTightFrameMACRoBPA {w}{1}{M}$. Let $\beta \in \SubtypeSet {x_{16}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\valRingCpMACRoFAF {p}}{x_{16}})}$. Then there exists a function $z$ from infinite places of $\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s}$ and elements of $J$ to elements of $\SubtypeSet {x_{15}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\valRingCpMACRoFAF {p}}{x_{15}})}$, such that for all infinite places $v$ of $\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s}$, for all elements $i$ of $J$, $\EqtMACRoDE {z (v , i)}{\beta \times \mathrm{PadicPlace\_\_\_u3b9\_\_u209a\_\_\_app} (\mathrm{starRingEnd\_\_app} (\ComplexMACRoAM , \mathrm{PlaceEmbedding\_\_\_u3a6\_\_\_app} (v , w (i))))}$ and for all sets $Q$, for all elements $x_{4}$ of $\mathrm{CommRing} (Q)$, if $p$ is characteristic, then for all elements $g$ of $\RingHomMACRoHO {(\SubtypeSet {x_{8}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\valRingCpMACRoFAF {p}}{x_{8}})})}{Q}$, if for all elements $x_{5}$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\valRingCpMACRoFAF {p}}{x_{7}})}$, if $\NormnormMACRoAK {x_{5}}\leq p ^ {\frac{ \mathrm{chiK\_\_app} (\ParametersdMACRoCIY , p)- 1}{2}}$, then $\EqtMACRoDE {g (x_{5})}{0}$, then for all elements $j$ of $\Natural$, if $j$ is odd or $p = \ParametersdMACRoCIY$, then for all infinite places $v_{1}$ and $v'$ of $\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{i_{1}}{(g (z (v_{1} , i_{1}))^ {p ^ {j}}\times g (z (v' , i_{1})))}}{\iteMACRoDD {(\EqtMACRoDE {(\ParametersplaceOfIntMACRoCJL {s}{\NegnegMACRoFV {(p ^ {j})}}\times v_{1})}{v'})}{(g (\beta)^ {p ^ {j}}\times g (\beta)\times M)}{0}}$.
Let (p/d) ≠ 1, (w_i) a tight frame of length M, β ∈ 𝔬. Then z_{v,i} = βι(w̄_{i,v}) ∈ 𝔬, and in characteristic p, modulo elements of valuation ≥ (1 − (p/d))/2, ∑_i z_{v,i}^{p^j} z_{v′,i} is β^{p^j+1}M if v′ = −p^j v, else 0 (j odd or p = d).
Proof. By Parameters.Φ, Parameters.frob_ext, Parameters.d, QuadField, valRingCp, norm_ringOfIntegers_le, Parameters.placeOfInt, Parameters.sqrtNegDInE, Parameters.chiK_eq_one_of_dvd, QuadField.sqrtNegDInt_sq, Parameters.α, Parameters.N₁_mul_α_mem, PadicPlace.ιₚ, finiteImage, Parameters.galOfPlaceInt, Parameters.N₁, algebraMap_mem_Ô, chiK_cases, PlaceEmbedding.Φ, Parameters.E, Parameters.phi_mul
and 12 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.mul_sum, neg_div, mul_mul_mul_comm, mul_assoc, IsUnit.dvd, IsLocalRing.residue_ne_zero_iff_isUnit, mul_right_comm, mul_comm, CharP.cast_eq_zero_iff, Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, Finset.sum_mul, MulMemClass.mul_mem
. ∎

Theorem 114.20src/New/Curve/TightFrameExists.lean: Parameters.exists_cyclotomic_different_factor
Let $R$ be a commutative ring. Let $N \in \Natural$. Assume that $R$ is an integral domain. Let $\zeta \in R$ such that $\mathrm{IsPrimitiveRoot} (\zeta , N)$. Let $l \in \SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\mathrm{Nat\_\_primeFactors} (N)}{x_{3}})}$. Then there exist natural numbers $r$ and $M$, such that $M \leq N = l \times r$, for all natural numbers $p$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\mathrm{Nat\_\_primeFactors} (N)}{p}$, then $\divides {p}{M}$, if and only if $p$ is distinct from $l$ and there exists an element $V$ of $R$, such that $\divides {V}{M}$ and $\EqtMACRoDE {\PolynomialevalMACRoGN {\zeta}{\mathrm{Polynomial\_\_derivative\_\_app} (\mathrm{Polynomial\_\_cyclotomic} (N , R))}\times (\zeta ^ {r}- 1)\times M}{N \times V}$.
Let ζ be a primitive N-th root of unity in a domain R of characteristic 0, l a prime factor of N = lr. Some M ≤ N, divisible by every prime factor of N but l, and some V dividing M in R have Φ_N′(ζ)(ζ^r − 1)M = NV.
Proof. By Parameters.expand_pow_cyclotomic
and 29 lemmas of Mathlib Polynomial.derivative_expand, Dvd.elim, Polynomial.cyclotomic_expand_eq_cyclotomic_mul, dvd_of_mul_right_dvd, Nat.Prime.one_lt, HasUniqueDiv.nsmul_right_injective, mul_dvd_mul_right, mul_assoc, Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq, Polynomial.eval_dvd, mul_dvd_mul_left, Dvd.intro_left, Nat.prime_eq_prime_of_dvd_pow, Polynomial.cyclotomic.dvd_X_pow_sub_one, Nat.Prime.one_le, Nat.Prime.ne_one, Nat.cast_mul, Polynomial.expand_mul, Polynomial.derivative_X_pow, Nat.exists_eq_pow_mul_and_not_dvd, pow_sub_one_mul, zero_lt_iff, Polynomial.IsRoot.eq_zero, Nat.Prime.dvd_mul, Polynomial.X_pow_sub_one_mul_cyclotomic_dvd_X_pow_sub_one_of_dvd, Nat.mem_primeFactors, Ne.bot_lt, Dvd.dvd.trans, IsPrimitiveRoot.isRoot_cyclotomic

Theorem 114.21src/New/Curve/TightFrameExists.lean: Parameters.exists_isTightFrame
Let $P$ be a parameter set. Let $s , p , m , R \in \Natural$ such that $p$ is prime, $\PadicPlaceMACRoCGW {p}$ and $(\ParametersdMACRoCIY \times \ParametersNuMACRoCIN {s})^ {6}\times (R + 1)\leq m$. Then there exists a set $I$, such that there exists an element $x$ of $\mathrm{Fintype} (I)$, such that there exists a function $v$ from $I$ to $\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s}$, such that there exists a function $c$ from $I$ to $\Natural$, such that $\IsTightFrameMACRoBPA {v}{c}{m}$, for all elements $i$ of $I$, $R \leq c (i)$ and there exists an element $i_{1}$ of $I$, such that for all infinite places $\tau$ of $\ParametersEMACRoCIJ {s}$, $\NormnormMACRoAK {\mathrm{PadicPlace\_\_\_u3b9\_\_u209a\_\_\_app} (\ParametersperiodFactorMACRoCJI {s}\times \mathrm{starRingEnd\_\_app} (\ComplexMACRoAM , \mathrm{PlaceEmbedding\_\_\_u3a6\_\_\_app} (\tau , v (i_{1}))))}= 1$.
Tight frames: for m ≥ (dN₁)⁶(R + 1) and ι: ℂ → ℂ_p there is a finite tight frame (v_i) of length m with multiplicities c_i ≥ R (`IsTightFrame`) in which, for some i, ι(s₁·conj τ(v_i)) is a p-adic unit at every place τ; s₁ = `Parameters.periodFactor`.
